Title: Relation sur le Quiétisme, par Messire Jacques Bénigne Bossuet, évêque de Meaux
Creator: Jacques-Bénigne Bossuet, bishop of Meaux
Publisher: A Paris, chez Jean Anisson, directeur de l'Imprimerie Royale, rue de la Harpe
Publication Date: 1698
Rights: Public Domain (published 1698, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item relationsurlequi00boss (https://archive.org/details/relationsurlequi00boss), the item's own relationsurlequi00boss_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John M Kelly Library Rare Books - University of Toronto; host metadata date: 1698. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 9d0df400b46d4fe0a85b081bacedc3804bc82642f4d4957cb2617c07ce51bac2.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of Toronto (John M. Kelly Library rare books) scan, digitised with University of Ottawa funding; good OCR, long s as f.
Content note: Bossuet's narrative of his dealings with Madame Guyon and Fénelon from 1693, written in 1698 to turn opinion against Fénelon, quoting Guyon's manuscripts and Fénelon's letters.
